`cross_corr_a_w(a_fft, w, n, N)`:

if N < 0: ...

g = conv[2*n-2::-1]
```

Pseudo loop:
```
for iter in time:
    A = rfft(a,N)
    c = irfft(A*A,N)[:2n-1]
    # maybe clip tiny negatives
    # exact max
    maxc = c.max()
    score = 2*n*maxc  # sum=1
    update best if score<best
    # weights from p-norm or LSE
    if p>1: pow = np.power(c, p-1, where=c>0); w=pow; wsum=w.sum(); if wsum>0 w/=wsum else break
    A? Need g before update using old A.
    g = cross using A,w
    gmax=g.max()
    if gmax<=0: break
    # try steps [step, step/2, ...]
    accepted=False
    factor_scale = g / gmax
    for sf in steps:
       a_try = a * np.exp(-sf * factor_scale)
       s_try = a_try.sum()
       if s_try<=0 or not finite: continue
       a_try /= s_try
       # compute new c, max
       At = rfft(a_try,N)
       ct = irfft(At*At,N)[:2n-1]
       mt = ct.max()
       st = 2*n*mt
       if st < best_score_iter? and st < score:
           a=a_try; c=ct; A=At; accepted=True; break
    if not accepted: # maybe stronger or perturb
       # if no decrease, try random perturbation of a (block) or reduce p
```

Let's design a more efficient custom optimizer:
Use p-norm objective and multiplicative update, with adaptive step based on `step_size` and no line search? Non-monotone may oscillate. We can use momentum and small step. Let's use a backtracking scheme but cache new c.

Start step = 0.3. For each p stage:
```
for it in range(max_iters):
  A=rfft(a,N); c=...
  # exact score, update best
  # objective val for rejection: p_norm = (sum(c**p))**(1/p)
  pow = np.power(c, p-1)
  sumpow = pow.sum()
  if not finite or sumpow==0: break
  w = pow / (sumpow)  # normalized sum? wsum =1
  g = cross(A,w,N,n)
  gmax = max(g)
  if gmax == 0: break
  # factor scale
  rel = g / gmax
  # try current step
  for sf in [step, step*0.5, step*0.25, step*0.125]:
      a_try = a * np.exp(-sf * rel)
      s = a_try.sum()
      if s==0: continue
      a_try /= s
      At=rfft(a_try,N); ct=irfft(At*At,N)[:m].real
      # clip small negatives
      mt=ct.max()
      st=2*n*mt
      # objective pnorm_try maybe
      if st < score - 1e-12:
          accept; break
  if not accepted:
      # If exact doesn't improve, maybe pnorm improved? Use pnorm criterion
      ...
      # If still not, reduce step *=0.5; if too small, perturb
```
This computes exact on candidate. If no acceptance and pnorm improved, maybe accept anyway to progress? But exact may temporarily increase. We can allow if `pnorm_try < pnorm - 1e-10`, even if exact not lower, and update best exact when it catches. This may help. But risk. Safer: accept if pnorm decreases and exact not too much worse (e.g., < score*1.05). We'll implement:
- If `st < score`: accept.
- Else if `pnorm_try < pnorm * (1 - 1e-8)` and `st < score * (1+0.05)`: accept (progress).
- Else no accept.
After no accept, reduce step; if step below 1e-4, add small random perturbation (multiply by exp(rng.normal(0,pgaussian amin)) and renorm) and continue; this helps escape.

Need compute pnorm = (sum(c**p))**(1/p). Instead of pow twice. For p integer, `c_pow = np.power(c,p)`. We can compute maybe expensive. Since n=10k, p up to32, okay. But for p=32, c^32 ~1e-128, finite. Use float64. `np.sum` maybe underflow if p large and c small, but max c ~1e-4, c^32 ~1e-128, sum ~1e-124, above min. p=128 ->1e-512 underflow. So p≤64 maybe. Use p list [2,4,8,16,24,32].

But if p=32, pnorm ~max c * (count* max^32 / sum)^(1/32) ≈max. Good.

For p=2, pnorm ~L2 norm of c, not exactly max; okay.

Gradient weights for p-norm: derivative of `pnorm^p = ∑c^p` is p∑c^{p-1} ∂c. We can use w=c^{p-1}. For p=2 w=c. Good. Normalize w sum to 1 for multiplicative scale; not necessary but convenient.

Potential issue: For sequences with zeros, c may have zeros; c^{p-1} for p=2 zero okay. For p>1, derivative zero at zero coefficients; no issue. If p non-integer no. We'll use integer.

Convergence: Multiplicative update decreases all coordinates by at most factor exp(-step). Since after renormalize, relative changes. Step maybe 0.2. For p=2 many iterations. We can set per p stage: run until time slice or no improvement for e.g. 2000 iterations. Or fixed max_iters based on time: cost per iter maybe. We can set `max_iters = 5000` per p? Total p 6 =>30k. Let's specify functions:

`next_pow2(x)`:
```
if x<=1: return 1
p=1
while p<x: p*=2
return p
```
`nlargest`? Use sort.

`normalize_array(a)`:
```
a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
s=a.sum()
if s<=0 or not finite: return None
return a/s
```
`fast_score(a)`:
```
n=len(a); s=a.sum()
if s<=0: return inf
# if n small use np.convolve? FFT okay
N=next_pow2(max(1,2*n-1))
A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:2*n-1]
c=np.maximum(c,0)
return float(2*n*max(c)/(s*s))
```
This computes with sum not normalized; objective scale invariant. For n=1, N=1, c length1. fine.
For score of normalized a, s=1.

`compute_convolution(a,N)`:
```
A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:len(a)+len(a)-1]
return A, np.maximum(c,0)
```
`compute_grad(a, A, w, n, N)`:
```
w_rev = np.zeros(N)
w_rev[:2*n-1] = w[::-1]
W = np.fft.rfft(w_rev, N)
conv = np.fft.irfft(A * W, N)
g = conv[2*n-2::-1]
return np.maximum(g,0)
```
Potential memory: `w_rev` length N each iteration. For N=16384, fine. Could allocate and fill.
Potential if N not >=3n-2? We choose N=next_pow2(3*n-2) in optimizer. For score N smaller but okay. optimize uses N>=3n-2.

`optimize_mult(a, N, seed, time_limit, p_list=(2,4,8,16,24), verbose)`:
- Copy a, normalize sum1 (but keep scale? objective scale invariant; use sum1 for internal). If a sum not1, a/=sum.
- `best = a.copy(); best_score=fast_score(a)` but fast_score uses N? We have c maybe. Use current score.
- `rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)`.
- `step = 0.35`
- For each p in p_list until time limit:
   - p_int = int(p)
   - `stage_best_score = current_score`
   - `no_improve=0`
   - `A,c = compute_convolution(a,N)`
   - `score = 2*n*c.max()`
   - while time.monotonic() < stage_deadline:
       update current best if score < best_score.
       # pnorm = (sum(c**p))**(1/p)
       # weights
       if p==2: w = c.copy(); wsum=w.sum()
       else: w = np.power(c, p-1); wsum=w.sum()
       if wsum <=0 or not finite: break
       w = w / wsum  # sum1
       g = compute_grad(a,A,w,n,N)
       gmax = g.max()
       if gmax <= 1e-300: break
       rel = g / gmax
       accepted=False
       # Maybe skip if rel uniform? if max-min < eps: perturb.
       # Try step values
       for faction in (1.0,0.6,0.35,0.2):
           sf = step * faction
           a_try = a * np.exp(-sf * rel)
           s = a_try.sum()
           if not finite or s <= 0: continue
           a_try /= s
           At, ct = compute_convolution(a_try,N)
           st = 2*n*ct.max()
           pnorm_try = pnorm_value(ct,p)
           if st < score - 1e-14:
               accept; break
           if pnorm_try < pnorm - 1e-12:
               # allow if exact not worse than 1.02 and maybe score lower than best_score?
               if st < max(score*1.02, best_score+0.01): accept; break
       if not accepted:
           # try random small perturbation if stagnant
           no_improve +=1
           step *= 0.65
           if step < 0.02:
               # perturb
               a = a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.12, n))
               a = a / a.sum()
               A,c = compute_convolution(a,N)
               score = 2*n*c.max()
               if score < best_score: best=copy
               step = 0.3
               no_improve=0
           else:
               if no_improve > 1000:
                   step=0.3; no_improve=0 # hmm
       else:
           a=a_try; A=At; c=ct; score=st
           if score < best_score: best=a.copy(); best_score=score; no_improve=0
           else: no_improve +=1? maybe if no exact improvement but p improved.
           # adapt step: if accepted first attempt, step = min(step*1.05, 1.0); else step = step*0.8
       # memory? no
   - after stage: a=best? Or keep current? Keep best so far.
```

`def fast_score(a):`
```
a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
n=a.size
if n==0: return np.inf
a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0); a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
s=float(a.sum())
if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s): return np.inf
# if n < 64 use direct np.convolve? FFT okay but direct simple.
N=next_pow2(2*n-1)
A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:2*n-1]
c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
mc=float(c.max())
return float(2.0*n*mc/(s*s))
```
For very large n maybe next_pow2(2n-1) huge; but we use <=10000.

`def convolution_fft(a, N=None):`
```

A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:2*n-1]
return A, np.maximum(c,0.0), N
```
`def gradient_fft(A, w, n, N):`
```
w_rev = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
w_rev[:2*n-1] = w[::-1]
W=np.fft.rfft(w_rev,N)

return np.maximum(g,0.0)
```
Could allocate w_rev once? Not necessary.

`def pnorm_weight_and_value(c, p):` returns w normalized, pnorm, wsum.
```
p=int(max(2,p))
if p==2:
    cpow = c.copy()
else:
    cpow = np.power(c, p-1, dtype? np.power supports)
wsum = float(cpow.sum())
if not finite or wsum<=0: return None, np.inf, 0
w = cpow / wsum
# pnorm = (wsum * dot(c,w))**(1/p)
val = float(wsum * np.dot(c,w))
if val<=0 or not finite: pnorm_val=np.inf
else: pnorm_val = val ** (1.0/float(p))
return w, pnorm_val, wsum
```
For c with zeros and p large, cpow 0 for zeros. For p=2 cpow c.
Potential `np.power` with integer exponent uses power? okay.

if not finite or s<=0: return None
a_try /= s
return a_try
```

`def resample_array(a, n):`
```
m=a.size
if m==1: return np.full(n, 1/n)

b=a[idx]
b=np.maximum(b,0); s=b.sum(); if s>0: b/=s
return b
```

```

`def select_top_candidates(cands, k, score_func):`

```

def generate_candidates(n, rng, extra_arrays=()):
    cands=[]
    i=np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    t = i/(max(1,n-1))
    for beta in [0.35,0.45,0.5]:
       base=np.power(i+1.0, -beta)
       for kappa in [0.0, 0.2, 0.5]:
           a=base*np.exp(-kappa*t)
           cands.append(a)
           # reversed
           cands.append(a[::-1].copy())
    for slope in [0.2,0.5,0.8]:
       a=1.0 - slope*t
       if a.min()<0: a=np.clip(a,1e-3, None)? Clipping changes shape better? Use a=np.maximum(a,1e-6)
       cands.append(a); cands.append(1.0 + slope*t)
    # power * oscillation
    base=np.power(i+1.0, -0.5)
    for amp in [0.1, 0.25]:
       for phase in [0.0, np.pi/2]:
           a=base*(1.0 + amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*t + phase))
           if a.min()>=0: cands.append(a)
    # random power modulation
    for _ in range(3): cands.append(base*np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.1,n)))
    # extra resample
    for arr in extra_arrays:
        if arr is not None and arr.size>0:
             a=np.interp(np.linspace(0, arr.size-1, n), np.arange(arr.size), arr)
             cands.append(a)
    # maybe height_sequence_1 added by caller
    normalized=[]
    for a in cands:
       b=np.asarray(a,dtype=float).ravel()
       b=np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0)
       b=np.clip(b,0,1000)
       s=b.sum()
       if s>0: normalized.append(b/s)
    return normalized
```

`pnorm_from_c` top-level:
```
def pnorm_from_c(c, p):
    p=int(p)
    if p<=0: return np.inf
    if p==1: return float(c.max())
    val = float(np.dot(c, np.power(c,p-1))) # sum c^p
    if not np.isfinite(val) or val<=0: return np.inf
    return val ** (1.0/float(p))
```
This powers each time. Could use `val = float(np.sum(np.power(c, p)))`. `dot(c, c**(p-1))` same. Fine.

Now, p stages: If p=2, pnorm is L2; if p=3, weights c^2. We can use p list [2,3,4,6,9,13,19]. Increasing. But too many. Let's choose `[2,3,5,8,12,17]` and time budget per stage. For pure power, p=2 may do shape; later sharpen.

`run_optimizer` implementation details:
```
def run_optimizer(a_init, n, deadline, seed, verbose=True):
    a = np.asarray(a_init, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    s = a.sum(); if s <=0: a=np.ones(n)/n
    else: a = a/s
    N = next_pow2(3*n - 2)
    A, c, _ = convolution_fft(a, N)
    score = 2.0*n*float(c.max())
    best = a.copy(); best_score=score
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    p_stages = (2,3,5,8,12,17)
    total_stages = len(p_stages)
    # Allocate stage budgets: if near deadline, adjust
    for idx,p in enumerate(p_stages):
        now=time.monotonic(); if now >= deadline: break
        remaining = deadline - now
        budget = min(remaining, max(2.0, remaining / float(total_stages - idx)))
        stage_deadline = now + budget
        step = 0.35
        no_improve = 0
        # ensure A,c current
        if a is not best? We maintain.
        while time.monotonic() < stage_deadline and no_improve < 3000:
            # current score from c (we should have c for current a)
            score = 2.0*n*float(np.max(c))
            if score < best_score:
                best_score=score; best=a.copy(); no_improve=0
            # weights
            w, pnorm, wsum = pnorm_weight_and_value(c, p)
            if w is None: break
            g = gradient_fft(A, w, n, N)
            gmax = float(g.max())
            if not np.isfinite(gmax) or gmax <= 1e-300:
                # random perturb
            else:
                rel = g / gmax
                accepted=False
                # Try 3 steps
                for fac in (1.0, 0.55, 0.3):
                   a_try = normalize_update(a, rel, step*fac)
                   if a_try is None: continue
                   At, ct, _ = convolution_fft(a_try, N)
                   st = 2.0*n*float(ct.max())
                   if st < score - 1e-15:
                       accept; accepted=True; chosen_fac=fac; break
                   pnorm_try = pnorm_from_c(ct,p)
                   if pnorm_try < pnorm * (1.0 - 1e-10) and st < max(score*1.05, best_score + 0.005):
                       accept; accepted=True; chosen_fac=fac; break
                if accepted:
                    a = a_try; A=At; c=ct
                    # adapt step
                    if chosen_fac == 1.0: step = min(0.9, step*1.05)
                    else: step = max(0.05, step*0.8)
                    no_improve = 0 if st < best_score + 1e-15 else min(no_improve+1, 500)
                else:
                    no_improve += 1
                    step = max(0.02, step*0.7)
                    if step <= 0.021 or no_improve > 1000:
                        # perturb: combine log random and block
                        a = random_perturb_log(a, rng, 0.18)
                        if n>1:
                            width = int(max(1, n//150))
                            st_idx = int(rng.integers(0, n-width+1))
                            a[st_idx:st_idx+width] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.35)))
                            a = np.clip(a,0,1000); s=a.sum();
                            if s>0: a=a/s
                        A,c,_ = convolution_fft(a,N)
                        step = 0.3
                        no_improve = 0
            # if no_improve large break stage
        # end while
        if best_score < score:
            a = best.copy()
            A,c,_ = convolution_fft(a,N)
    return best, float(best_score)
```
Need ensure `score` defined before assign in last if. We can update each loop.

Now, `polish_sequence(a, deadline, seed)`:
```
def polish_sequence(a, deadline, seed):
    n=a.size; a=normalize(a)
    N=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    best=a.copy(); best_score=fast_score_with_N(a,N,n)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    patience=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       cand=a.copy()
       mode = rng.random()
       if mode<0.5:
          width=int(rng.integers(max(1,n//400), max(2,n//20)))
          start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width+1))
          sigma=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.4))
          cand[start:start+width] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,sigma)))
       else:
          sigma=float(rng.uniform(0.03,0.15))
          cand *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,sigma,n))
       cand=np.clip(cand,0,1000); s=cand.sum(); if s<=0: continue; cand/=s
       sc=fast_score_with_N(cand,N,n)
       if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
           best=cand.copy(); best_score=sc; a=cand; patience=0
       else:
           patience+=1
           if patience>500:
              # perturb a to escape
              a = random_perturb_log(a, rng, 0.2)
              patience=0
    return best, best_score
```
This only accepts improvements, so `a` is best always. If stuck, perturb `a` but keep `best`; after perturbation, need update a to perturbed and continue, best remains separate. Good. But if we perturb `a` while not updating best, subsequent candidates from perturbed may or may not improve best. Fine.

Need `fast_score_with_N(a, N, n)` top-level:
```
def fast_score_with_N(a,N,n):
    s=a.sum(); if s<=0: return inf
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:2*n-1]
    return 2*n*float(np.max(c))/(s*s)
```

Let's write CandidateStore:
```
class CandidateStore:
    def __init__(self, limit=40):
        self.limit=limit
        self.items=[]
        self.best_fast=np.inf
    def add(self, a):
        if a is None: return
        arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if arr.size==0: return
        arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr),arr,0.0)
        arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
        s=arr.sum()
        if s<=0: return
        arr=arr/s
        fs=fast_score(arr)
        if not np.isfinite(fs): return
        self.items.append((float(fs), arr.copy()))
        if len(self.items)>self.limit:
            self.items.sort(key=pair_first)
            self.items = self.items[:self.limit]
        if fs < self.best_fast:
            self.best_fast=fs
    def top_arrays(self, k=10):
        self.items.sort(key=pair_first)
        return [pair[1] for pair in self.items[:k]]
```
No lambdas. Pair_first top-level.

Potential memory: store arrays each length n. 40*4806*8~1.5MB; fine.

`random_perturb_log` top-level uses rng, returns normalized. Need handle n=0.
```
def random_perturb_log(a, rng, sigma=0.1):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if a.size==0: return a
    a=a*np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, a.size))
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000); s=a.sum(); if s<=0: return np.ones_like(a)/a.size; return a/s
```

if gamma<=0: right=np.ones(n)
else: right=np.power(n - i, -gamma); sr=right.sum(); right/= sr
a=(1.0-lam)*left + lam*right
s=a.sum(); if s<=0: return None; return a/s
```

Need `resample_to_n(a,n)` top-level:
```
def resample_to_n(a,n):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float).ravel(); m=a.size
   if m==0: return None
   if m==1: b=np.full(n, a[0])
   else: b=np.interp(np.linspace(0,m-1,n), np.arange(m), a)
   b=np.clip(b,0,1000); s=b.sum(); if s<=0: return None; return b/s
```

Let's write pnorm_weight robust:
```
def pnorm_weight_and_value(c, p):
    p=int(max(2,p))
    if p==2:
       cpow=c.copy()
    else:
       with np.errstate(over='ignore', under='ignore', invalid='ignore'):
           cpow=np.power(c, p-1)
    cpow=np.where(np.isfinite(cpow), cpow, 0.0)
    wsum=float(cpow.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(wsum) or wsum<=0:
       # fallback c
       cpow=c.copy(); wsum=float(cpow.sum())
       if not finite or <=0: return None, np.inf, 0.0
    w=cpow/wsum
    val = float(wsum * np.dot(c, w))
    if not finite or val<=0: pnorm_val=np.inf
    else: pnorm_val = val ** (1.0/float(p))
    return w,pnorm_val,wsum
```

```python
"""
Search algorithm:
- Starts from many asymmetric, one-sided power-law / mixed endpoint motifs.
- Uses a multiplicative p-norm descent on the convolution coefficients.
- Periodically resamples smaller profiles to larger lengths and polishes the best
  sequence with local block perturbations.
- Returns the best exact-evaluated sequence before the time budget ends.
"""

import time
import numpy as np


class CandidateStore:
    """Small pool of candidate arrays, ranked by fast score."""

    def __init__(self, limit=70):
        self.limit = int(limit)
        self.items = []

    def add(self, a):
        if a is None:
            return
        arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if arr.size == 0:
            return
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(arr.sum())
        if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
            return
        arr = arr / s
        fs = fast_score(arr)
        if not np.isfinite(fs):
            return
        self.items.append((float(fs), arr.copy()))
        if len(self.items) > self.limit:
            self.items.sort(key=pair_first)
            self.items = self.items[:self.limit]

    def top_arrays(self, k=12):
        self.items.sort(key=pair_first)
        return [it[1] for it in self.items[:int(k)]]


def pair_first(item):
    return item[0]


def next_pow2(x):
    x = int(max(1, x))
    p = 1
    while p < x:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def make_uniform(n):
    return np.ones(int(n), dtype=np.float64) / float(int(n))


def normalize_array(a, floor=0.0):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, floor, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return None
    return arr / s


def resample_to_n(a, n):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(n)
    if arr.size == 0 or n <= 0:
        return None
    if arr.size == 1:
        b = np.full(n, float(arr[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        x_old = np.arange(arr.size, dtype=np.float64)
        x_new = np.linspace(0.0, float(arr.size - 1), n, dtype=np.float64)
        b = np.interp(x_new, x_old, arr, left=float(arr[0]), right=float(arr[-1]))
    b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
    b = np.clip(b, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(b.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return None
    return b / s


def make_power_mixed(n, beta=0.5, gamma=0.0, lam=0.0):
    """
    (1 - lam) * normalized left power law i^-beta
    + lam * normalized right power law (n-i)^-gamma
    """
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    left = np.power(i + 1.0, float(-beta))
    sl = float(left.sum())
    if sl <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(sl):
        return None
    left /= sl

    if abs(float(gamma)) < 1e-12:
        right = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        right = np.power((float(n) - i).astype(np.float64), float(-gamma))
        sr = float(right.sum())
        if sr <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(sr):
            right = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            right /= sr

    lam = float(np.clip(lam, 0.0, 1.0))
    a = (1.0 - lam) * left + lam * right
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return None
    return a / s


def generate_candidates(n, rng, extra_arrays=()):
    n = int(n)
    out = []
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = i / float(max(1, n - 1))

    # Main explicit family: mixed left/right power laws.
    for beta in (0.40, 0.45, 0.50):
        for gamma in (0.0, 0.15, 0.25, 0.35, 0.45):
            for lam in (0.0, 0.10, 0.20, 0.35, 0.50):
                a = make_power_mixed(n, beta, gamma, lam)
                if a is not None:
                    out.append(a)

    # Linear trends, clipped with a small floor to keep positive.
    for slope in (0.20, 0.45, 0.75):
        a = 1.0 - slope * t
        a = np.maximum(a, 1e-4)
        a = normalize_array(a)
        if a is not None:
            out.append(a)

        a = 1.0 + slope * t
        a = normalize_array(a)
        if a is not None:
            out.append(a)

    # Mild oscillations on the optimal beta power law.
    base = np.power(i + 1.0, -0.5)
    for amp in (0.08, 0.18):
        for phase in (0.0, 0.5 * np.pi):
            a = base * (1.0 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t + phase))
            a = np.maximum(a, 1e-6)
            a = normalize_array(a)
            if a is not None:
                out.append(a)

    # Random smooth modulations of the power law.
    for _ in range(4):
        a = base * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.12, n))
        a = np.maximum(a, 1e-6)
        a = normalize_array(a)
        if a is not None:
            out.append(a)

    # Resample any external profiles.
    for ex in extra_arrays:
        if ex is None:
            continue
        b = resample_to_n(ex, n)
        if b is not None:
            out.append(b)

    cleaned = []
    for a in out:
        b = normalize_array(a)
        if b is not None:
            cleaned.append(b)
    return cleaned


def select_top_arrays(cands, k=3):
    scored = []
    for a in cands:
        if a is None:
            continue
        b = normalize_array(a)
        if b is None:
            continue
        fs = fast_score(b)
        if np.isfinite(fs):
            scored.append((fs, b))
    scored.sort(key=pair_first)
    return [it[1] for it in scored[:int(k)]]


def fast_score(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(arr.size)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return float("inf")
    N = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    A = np.fft.rfft(arr, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(c)) / (s * s))


def fast_score_with_N(a, N, n):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return float("inf")
    A = np.fft.rfft(arr, int(N))
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, int(N))[:2 * int(n) - 1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return float(2.0 * int(n) * float(np.max(c)) / (s * s))


def convolution_fft(a, N=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if N is None:
        N = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    N = int(N)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:2 * n - 1]
    return A, np.maximum(c, 0.0), N


def gradient_fft(A, w, n, N, w_rev):
    """
    Given w_k (length 2n-1), compute
    g_i = sum_{t=0}^{n-1} a_t * w_{i+t}
    using A = rfft(a).
    """
    n = int(n)
    N = int(N)
    w_rev.fill(0.0)
    w_rev[:2 * n - 1] = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()[:2 * n - 1][::-1]
    W = np.fft.rfft(w_rev, N)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(A * W, N)
    g = conv[2 * n - 2::-1]
    return np.maximum(g, 0.0)


def pnorm_weight_and_value(c, p):
    p = int(max(2, p))
    if p == 2:
        cpow = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    else:
        with np.errstate(over="ignore", under="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
            cpow = np.power(c, p - 1)
        cpow = np.where(np.isfinite(cpow), cpow, 0.0)

    wsum = float(cpow.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(wsum) or wsum <= 0.0:
        cpow = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        wsum = float(cpow.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(wsum) or wsum <= 0.0:
            return None, float("inf"), 0.0

    w = cpow / wsum
    val = float(wsum * float(np.dot(c, w)))
    if not np.isfinite(val) or val <= 0.0:
        pnorm = float("inf")
    else:
        pnorm = float(val ** (1.0 / float(p)))
    return w, pnorm, wsum


def pnorm_from_c(c, p):
    p = int(max(2, p))
    if p == 2:
        val = float(np.dot(c, c))
    else:
        with np.errstate(over="ignore", under="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
            val = float(np.dot(c, np.power(c, p - 1)))
    if not np.isfinite(val) or val <= 0.0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(val ** (1.0 / float(p)))


def normalize_update(a, rel, step):
    if float(step) <= 0.0:
        return None
    a_try = a * np.exp(-float(step) * rel)
    s = float(a_try.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None
    return a_try / s


def random_perturb_log(a, rng, sigma=0.15):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    a = a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, float(sigma), a.size))
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0) + 1e-12
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return make_uniform(a.size)
    return a / s


def run_optimizer(a_init, deadline, seed=12345):
    """
    Multiplicative p-norm descent.
    Returns (best_array, best_fast_score).
    """
    a = np.asarray(a_init, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return None, float("inf")

    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        a = make_uniform(n)
    else:
        a = a / s

    N = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    A, c, N = convolution_fft(a, N)
    score = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = score
    w_rev = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    p_stages = (2, 3, 5, 8, 12, 17)
    num_stages = len(p_stages)

    for idx, p in enumerate(p_stages):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= float(deadline):
            break
        remaining = float(deadline) - now
        stage_budget = max(2.0, remaining / float(num_stages - idx))
        stage_deadline = min(float(deadline), now + stage_budget)

        # Start each stage from the best found so far.
        if best_score < score:
            a = best.copy()
            A, c, _ = convolution_fft(a, N)
            score = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))

        step = 0.35
        no_improve = 0
        max_no_improve = 2500

        while time.monotonic() < stage_deadline and no_improve < max_no_improve:
            if c.size == 0:
                break
            score = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))
            if not np.isfinite(score):
                break
            if score < best_score:
                best_score = score
                best = a.copy()
                no_improve = 0

            w, pnorm, wsum = pnorm_weight_and_value(c, p)
            if w is None:
                w = c / max(1e-300, float(c.sum()))
                pnorm = pnorm_from_c(c, p)
                wsum = float(c.sum())

            g = gradient_fft(A, w, n, N, w_rev)
            gmax = float(np.max(g))
            if not np.isfinite(gmax) or gmax <= 1e-300:
                # Nothing to move; perturb from best.
                a = random_perturb_log(best.copy(), rng, 0.20)
                A, c, _ = convolution_fft(a, N)
                score = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))
                step = 0.35
                no_improve = 0
                if score < best_score:
                    best_score = score
                    best = a.copy()
                continue

            rel = g / gmax
            accepted = False
            chosen_fac = 1.0

            for fac in (1.0, 0.55, 0.30, 0.15):
                sf = step * fac
                a_try = normalize_update(a, rel, sf)
                if a_try is None:
                    continue

                At, ct, _ = convolution_fft(a_try, N)
                st = float(2.0 * n * np.max(ct))
                if not np.isfinite(st):
                    continue

                if st < score - 1e-14:
                    accepted = True
                    chosen_fac = fac
                    break

                pnorm_try = pnorm_from_c(ct, p)
                if (np.isfinite(pnorm_try) and pnorm_try < pnorm * (1.0 - 1e-11)
                        and st < max(score * 1.04, best_score + 0.004)):
                    accepted = True
                    chosen_fac = fac
                    break

            if accepted:
                a = a_try
                A = At
                c = ct
                score = st
                if chosen_fac >= 1.0:
                    step = min(0.90, step * 1.08)
                else:
                    step = max(0.035, step * 0.82)
                if score < best_score + 1e-13:
                    no_improve = 0
                else:
                    no_improve += 1
            else:
                no_improve += 1
                step = max(0.015, step * 0.70)
                if step <= 0.02 or no_improve > 1200:
                    # Restart from best with a random asymmetric kick.
                    a = random_perturb_log(best.copy(), rng, 0.18)
                    width = int(max(1, n // 180))
                    start = int(rng.integers(0, n - width + 1))
                    a[start:start + width] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.35)))
                    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0) + 1e-12
                    s2 = float(a.sum())
                    if s2 > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s2):
                        a = a / s2
                    else:
                        a = best.copy()
                    A, c, _ = convolution_fft(a, N)
                    score = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))
                    step = 0.30
                    no_improve = 0
                    if score < best_score:
                        best_score = score
                        best = a.copy()

        # End of p stage: if best improved during the stage, continue from it.
        if best_score < score:
            a = best.copy()
            A, c, _ = convolution_fft(a, N)
            score = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))

    return best, best_score


def polish_sequence(a, deadline, seed=777):
    """Local block/random multiplicative polish, accepting only fast-score decreases."""
    arr = normalize_array(a)
    if arr is None:
        return None, float("inf")
    n = int(arr.size)
    N = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)

    best = arr.copy()
    best_score = fast_score_with_N(best, N, n)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    patience = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        cand = arr.copy()
        mode = float(rng.random())

        if mode < 0.55:
            lo = max(1, n // 400)
            hi = max(lo + 1, n // 16)
            if hi <= n:
                width = int(rng.integers(lo, hi))
                start = int(rng.integers(0, n - width + 1))
                sigma = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.38))
                cand[start:start + width] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma)))
        else:
            sigma = float(rng.uniform(0.03, 0.14))
            cand *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n))

        cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0) + 1e-12
        cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(cand.sum())
        if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
            patience += 1
            if patience > 700:
                arr = random_perturb_log(best.copy(), rng, 0.22)
                patience = 0
            continue
        cand = cand / s

        sc = fast_score_with_N(cand, N, n)
        if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best = cand.copy()
            best_score = sc
            arr = cand
            patience = 0
        else:
            patience += 1
            if patience > 700:
                arr = random_perturb_log(best.copy(), rng, 0.22)
                patience = 0

    return best, best_score


def exact_score_from_array(a):
    try:
        seq = [float(x) for x in np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()]
        val = float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
        return val
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0, **kwargs):
    start = time.monotonic()
    total = max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = start + max(0.0, total - 8.0)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    store = CandidateStore(limit=90)

    # Fallback: one-sided square-root power law at length 4806.
    fallback_n = 4806
    fallback = make_power_mixed(fallback_n, 0.5, 0.0, 0.0)
    if fallback is not None:
        store.add(fallback)

    fy = 0.0
    if fallback is not None:
        fy = fast_score(fallback)
    print(f"[propose] fallback fast score: {fy:.10f} n={fallback_n}", flush=True)

    # Determine a large working length from the supplied previous construction if sane.
    large_n = 4806
    height_arr = None
    try:
        hs = height_sequence_1
        h = np.asarray(hs, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if 1200 <= int(h.size) <= 12000:
            h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
            h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1000.0)
            sh = float(h.sum())
            if sh > 0.01 and np.isfinite(sh):
                height_arr = h / sh
                large_n = int(h.size)
                store.add(height_arr)
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Small-scale profile search: cheap, useful for resampling to large n.
    small_n = 256
    if total > 900:
        small_budget = min(240.0, 0.12 * total)
        small_deadline = min(deadline, start + small_budget)
        try:
            small_cands = generate_candidates(small_n, rng, extra_arrays=())
            small_top = select_top_arrays(small_cands, 2)
            if small_top:
                small_best_small, small_sc = run_optimizer(small_top[0], small_deadline, seed + 101)
                if small_best_small is not None:
                    store.add(small_best_small)
                if len(small_top) > 1 and time.monotonic() < small_deadline:
                    sub_dead = min(small_deadline, time.monotonic() + 0.35 * max(0.0, small_deadline - time.monotonic()))
                    small_best2, _ = run_optimizer(small_top[1], sub_dead, seed + 202)
                    if small_best2 is not None:
                        store.add(small_best2)
        except Exception as exc:
            print("[propose] small stage failed:", type(exc).__name__, flush=True)

    # Large-scale explicit candidates and optimized refinement.
    try:
        extra_arrays = []
        if height_arr is not None:
            extra_arrays.append(height_arr)
        if store.items:
            # Use current best small/large profile as an extra seed.
            current_top = store.top_arrays(1)
            if current_top:
                extra_arrays.append(current_top[0])

        large_cands = generate_candidates(large_n, rng, extra_arrays=extra_arrays)
        large_top = select_top_arrays(large_cands, 3)
        for arr in large_top:
            store.add(arr)

        # Ensure a strong pure-power large candidate is present.
        store.add(make_power_mixed(large_n, 0.5, 0.0, 0.0))

        large_main_deadline = min(deadline, start + max(0.0, total - 70.0))
        if large_top and time.monotonic() < large_main_deadline:
            remaining_to_main = max(0.0, large_main_deadline - time.monotonic())
            first_dead = min(large_main_deadline, time.monotonic() + 0.72 * remaining_to_main)
            resA, scA = run_optimizer(large_top[0], first_dead, seed + 303)
            if resA is not None:
                store.add(resA)
            print(f"[propose] large run A fast: {scA:.10f} n={large_n}", flush=True)

            if time.monotonic() < large_main_deadline - 30.0 and len(large_top) > 1:
                rem2 = large_main_deadline - time.monotonic()
                second_dead = min(large_main_deadline, time.monotonic() + 0.42 * rem2)
                resB, scB = run_optimizer(large_top[1], second_dead, seed + 404)
                if resB is not None:
                    store.add(resB)
                print(f"[propose] large run B fast: {scB:.10f} n={large_n}", flush=True)

            if time.monotonic() < large_main_deadline - 60.0 and len(large_top) > 2:
                rem3 = large_main_deadline - time.monotonic()
                third_dead = min(large_main_deadline, time.monotonic() + 0.30 * rem3)
                resC, scC = run_optimizer(large_top[2], third_dead, seed + 505)
                if resC is not None:
                    store.add(resC)
                print(f"[propose] large run C fast: {scC:.10f} n={large_n}", flush=True)
    except Exception as exc:
        print("[propose] large stage failed:", type(exc).__name__, flush=True)

    # Polish the currently best array.
    try:
        top1 = store.top_arrays(1)
        if top1:
            polish_deadline = max(time.monotonic() + 1.0, min(deadline, start + total - 2.0))
            parr = top1[0]
            # Prefer a large length for final polishing; resampling is cheap.
            if int(parr.size) != large_n and int(parr.size) < 1500:
                parr = resample_to_n(parr, large_n)
            polished, psc = polish_sequence(parr, polish_deadline, seed + 606)
            if polished is not None:
                store.add(polished)
            print(f"[propose] polish fast: {psc:.10f} n={int(polished.size) if polished is not None else 0}", flush=True)
    except Exception as exc:
        print("[propose] polish failed:", type(exc).__name__, flush=True)

    # Exact-evaluate a few best candidates and return the true best.
    best_arr = None
    best_exact = float("inf")
    top_candidates = store.top_arrays(22)
    if not top_candidates and fallback is not None:
        top_candidates = [fallback]

    eval_deadline = start + max(0.0, total - 2.0)
    for arr in top_candidates:
        if time.monotonic() >= eval_deadline:
            break
        val = exact_score_from_array(arr)
        if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_exact:
            best_exact = val
            best_arr = arr

    if best_arr is None:
        if top_candidates:
            best_arr = top_candidates[0]
        elif fallback is not None:
            best_arr = fallback
        else:
            best_arr = make_uniform(1)

    final_arr = np.asarray(best_arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    final_arr = np.where(np.isfinite(final_arr), final_arr, 0.0)
    final_arr = np.clip(final_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(final_arr.sum())
    if s <= 0.01 or not np.isfinite(s):
        final_arr = make_uniform(1)

    # A safe scale: sum 1.0 is enough and keeps every entry far below 1000.
    if final_arr.size and float(final_arr.sum()) > 0:
        final_arr = final_arr / float(final_arr.sum())

    attempt = [float(x) for x in final_arr]
    try:
        v = float(evaluate_sequence(attempt))
        if not np.isfinite(v):
            fallback_seqs = make_power_mixed(fallback_n, 0.5, 0.0, 0.0)
            attempt = [float(1.0)] if fallback_seqs is None else [float(x) for x in fallback_seqs]
    except Exception:
        pass

    return attempt
```
